<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://john.fun/elevators" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Elevators</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Elevators</span></a><span class="dom">john.fun</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN project called Elevators presents a novel approach or tool related to elevator systems—either scheduling, simulation, or a microservice concept—demonstrating practical, small-scale engineering work.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Elevators 项目展示了一个与电梯相关的工具/演示，可能是调度、仿真或小型工程原型，欢迎社区反馈与迭代。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN project called Elevators presents a novel approach or tool related to elevator systems—either scheduling, simulation, or a microservice concept—demonstrating practical, small-scale engineering work. It invites community feedback and iteration.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Elevators 项目展示了一个与电梯相关的工具/演示，可能是调度、仿真或小型工程原型，欢迎社区反馈与迭代。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K3" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kimi-K3 on HuggingFace</span><span class='zh'>Kimi-K3 在 HuggingFace 上</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kimi-K3 is now available on HuggingFace, enabling researchers and developers to try, fine-tune, and benchmark the model with familiar tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi-K3 已在 HuggingFace 上公开，可供研究者和开发者直接试用与微调。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kimi-K3 is now available on HuggingFace, enabling researchers and developers to try, fine-tune, and benchmark the model with familiar tooling. The release underscores the ongoing push to open-source options and community collaboration around language models on popular platforms.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi-K3 已在 HuggingFace 上公开，可供研究者和开发者直接试用与微调。此举进一步推动开源模型在 HuggingFace 生态中的扩展，便于社区贡献和基准测试。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techspot.com/news/113236-us-prosecutors-charge-atlanta-man-after-grapheneos-phone.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US citizen charged after GrapheneOS phone wipes during airport search</span><span class='zh'>美国公民在机场安检时因 GrapheneOS 自动清除数据而被起诉</span></a><span class="dom">techspot.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The case highlights how privacy-focused devices like GrapheneOS can trigger data wipe during border checks, leading to legal action.</span><span class='zh'>一起案例显示隐私导向设备在机场安检时可能因安全特性触发数据擦除，进而引发起诉。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The case highlights how privacy-focused devices like GrapheneOS can trigger data wipe during border checks, leading to legal action. It underscores tensions between personal data protection, law enforcement, and the risk of unintended data loss.</span><span class='zh'>一起案例显示隐私导向设备在机场安检时可能因安全特性触发数据擦除，进而引发起诉。凸显了个人数据保护、执法权力与意外数据丢失之间的张力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.uefa.com/news-media/news/02a7-213a92896eb0-54dfbf454e3b-1000--statement-on-behalf-of-uefa-and-its-55-national-associations/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>UEFA and its national associations will not participate in FIFA competitions</span><span class='zh'>欧足联及55个成员协会宣布退出FIFA赛事</span></a><span class="dom">uefa.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>UEFA and 55 national associations announced they will not participate in FIFA competitions, signaling a governance dispute between regional bodies and FIFA.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲足联与55个成员协会宣布不参与FIFA赛事，标志着与FIFA在治理与日程安排上的公开分歧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>UEFA and 55 national associations announced they will not participate in FIFA competitions, signaling a governance dispute between regional bodies and FIFA. The move could disrupt the international calendar and pressure FIFA to concede on reform terms.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲足联与55个成员协会宣布不参与FIFA赛事，标志着与FIFA在治理与日程安排上的公开分歧。此举可能扰乱国际日历，促使对改革条款的谈判加剧。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/position-open-weights-models" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/62993bb857986e2808c2a75024f384e7b85312b6-2400x1254.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='anthropic.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/position-open-weights-models" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Our position on open-weights models</span><span class='zh'>关于开放权重模型的立场</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic outlines its stance on open-weights models, emphasizing safety, governance, and responsible sharing over unrestricted release.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 阐述对开放权重模型的立场，强调在安全、治理与负责任共享之间取得平衡，而非盲目公开。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic outlines its stance on open-weights models, emphasizing safety, governance, and responsible sharing over unrestricted release. It discusses the risk of misuse and proposes criteria for when and how weights should be shared or accessed.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 阐述对开放权重模型的立场，强调在安全、治理与负责任共享之间取得平衡，而非盲目公开。文中指出若在缺乏防护的情况下公开权重可能被滥用，并提出负责任共享与准入控制的标准。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://killthecookiebanner.eu/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=killthecookiebanner.eu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>killthecookiebanner.eu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://killthecookiebanner.eu/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kill The Cookie Banner</span><span class='zh'>抵制 Cookie 横幅计划</span></a><span class="dom">killthecookiebanner.eu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A privacy-focused initiative that advocates simpler consent UX and fewer cookie banners, potentially proposing standards or tools to reduce intrusive tracking prompts.</span><span class='zh'>该计划推动更简洁的同意界面与更少的 Cookie 横幅，提出降低追踪提示的标准或工具，改善浏览体验的同时保持隐私保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A privacy-focused initiative that advocates simpler consent UX and fewer cookie banners, potentially proposing standards or tools to reduce intrusive tracking prompts. This movement aims to improve browsing experience while preserving essential privacy controls.</span><span class='zh'>该计划推动更简洁的同意界面与更少的 Cookie 横幅，提出降低追踪提示的标准或工具，改善浏览体验的同时保持隐私保护。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>1021</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49057175' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>488</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-49063754" data-id="49063754" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Data" data-score="912" data-comments="89" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063754" data-time="1785111544">
<a class="thumb" href="https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/og.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nikolays.github.io' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PGSimCity - How PostgreSQL Works</span><span class='zh'>PGSimCity：PostgreSQL 的工作原理</span></a><span class="dom">nikolays.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PGSimCity uses a city-building metaphor to illuminate PostgreSQL internals, from storage and indexing to the query planner and executor.</span><span class='zh'>PGSimCity 以城市场景比喻讲解 PostgreSQL 的内部结构，包括存储、索引、查询优化器和执行器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PGSimCity uses a city-building metaphor to illuminate PostgreSQL internals, from storage and indexing to the query planner and executor. It helps readers understand how MVCC, WAL, and background processes combine to make PostgreSQL reliable and scalable.</span><span class='zh'>PGSimCity 以城市场景比喻讲解 PostgreSQL 的内部结构，包括存储、索引、查询优化器和执行器。帮助读者理解 MVCC、WAL 等机制是如何协同工作，提升可靠性与可扩展性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/07/read-this-before-you-buy-that-tv-streaming-stick/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Read this before you buy that TV streaming stick</span><span class='zh'>买电视流媒体棒前该知道的事</span></a><span class="dom">krebsonsecurity.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Smart TV sticks often ship with limited security and privacy protections, making them an easy attack vector for malware and data leakage.</span><span class='zh'>电视流媒体棒常常安全与隐私保护不足，容易成为恶意软件与数据泄露的入口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Smart TV sticks often ship with limited security and privacy protections, making them an easy attack vector for malware and data leakage. When shopping, prioritize reputable brands, timely firmware updates, and configurable privacy settings to minimize risk.</span><span class='zh'>电视流媒体棒常常安全与隐私保护不足，容易成为恶意软件与数据泄露的入口。选购时应关注品牌信誉、固件更新及时性以及可配置的隐私设置，以降低风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/HedgieMarkets/status/2081534588485296565" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI companies are shredding rare books</span><span class='zh'>AI公司正在撕碎珍稀书籍</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reports claim AI companies are shredding rare books to feed training data, raising alarms about data sourcing and cultural preservation.</span><span class='zh'>有报道称AI公司在收集训练数据时撕碎珍稀书籍，这引发对数据来源与文化保护的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reports claim AI companies are shredding rare books to feed training data, raising alarms about data sourcing and cultural preservation. The trend highlights the tension between rapid AI development and safeguarding irreplaceable artifacts, prompting calls for governance and licensing reforms. Experts warn the losses could be irreversible if these materials are discarded before proper archival decisions are made.</span><span class='zh'>有报道称AI公司在收集训练数据时撕碎珍稀书籍，这引发对数据来源与文化保护的担忧。此趋势突显在推动AI高速发展时保护不可替代文献的难题，促使推动治理与许可框架的改革。若属实，损失可能是不可逆的，相关机构被敦促做出更审慎的档案保存决策。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>771</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49068738' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>491</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49112232" data-id="49112232" data-tags="Programming|DevOps" data-score="767" data-comments="290" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112232" data-time="1785428776">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">14</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.blog/changelog/2026-07-30-stacked-pull-requests-are-now-in-public-preview/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs are now live on GitHub</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 现已上线堆叠 PR（Stacked PRs）</span></a><span class="dom">github.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs enable multiple dependent pull requests to be submitted and reviewed together, currently in public preview.</span><span class='zh'>堆叠 PR 允许将相互依赖的多个合并请求打包提交与评审，现处于公开预览阶段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs enable multiple dependent pull requests to be submitted and reviewed together, currently in public preview. This workflow can streamline complex changes, reduce review churn, and improve collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>堆叠 PR 允许将相互依赖的多个合并请求打包提交与评审，现处于公开预览阶段。该工作流有助于简化复杂改动、降低评审成本、提升协作效率。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://earendil.com/posts/session-portability/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://earendil.com/static/og/posts/session-portability.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='earendil.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://earendil.com/posts/session-portability/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The session you cannot take with you</span><span class='zh'>你带不走的会话</span></a><span class="dom">earendil.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Session portability is a growing problem as users expect to pick up where they left off across devices.</span><span class='zh'>随着用户期望在多设备间无缝继续工作，会话可移植性成为一个日益突出的问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Session portability is a growing problem as users expect to pick up where they left off across devices. The piece surveys approaches like portable session tokens, device linking, and cryptographic handoffs, weighing security and UX trade-offs. The takeaway is that truly portable sessions could unlock seamless multi-device experiences.</span><span class='zh'>随着用户期望在多设备间无缝继续工作，会话可移植性成为一个日益突出的问题。文中探讨了可移植会话令牌、设备绑定与密钥交接等方案，并权衡安全性与用户体验的取舍。真正的会话可移植性或将解锁无缝的多设备使用体验。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>751</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49118781' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>213</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49095865" data-id="49095865" data-tags="Open Source|Privacy" data-score="720" data-comments="232" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49095865" data-time="1785323108">
<a class="thumb" href="https://koreader.rocks/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=koreader.rocks&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>koreader.rocks</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">16</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://koreader.rocks/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>KOReader</span><span class='zh'>KOReader：跨平台的开源电子书阅读器</span></a><span class="dom">koreader.rocks</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KOReader remains a robust, open-source e-reader that supports a wide range of formats and platforms, including e-ink devices.</span><span class='zh'>KOReader 依然是一款强大的跨平台开源电子书阅读器，支持多种格式与设备，包括电子墨水设备。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KOReader remains a robust, open-source e-reader that supports a wide range of formats and platforms, including e-ink devices. It emphasizes customization, annotations, and privacy-friendly features not always available in closed apps.</span><span class='zh'>KOReader 依然是一款强大的跨平台开源电子书阅读器，支持多种格式与设备，包括电子墨水设备。它强调可定制、注释功能以及在封闭应用中不易实现的隐私友好特性。文章凸显了KOReader如何继续赋能读者，让用户对设备拥有更多控制权。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/yc-software/qm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>qm – Multiplayer agent harness for work</span><span class='zh'>qm：面向工作场景的多人代理框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>qm is an open-source harness that coordinates multiple agents to work on tasks collaboratively.</span><span class='zh'>qm 是一个开源的多人代理协调框架，支持在工作场景中让多个代理协同完成任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>qm is an open-source harness that coordinates multiple agents to work on tasks collaboratively. It provides orchestration, tasking, and visibility across agent-driven workflows, helping teams tackle complex problems more efficiently.</span><span class='zh'>qm 是一个开源的多人代理协调框架，支持在工作场景中让多个代理协同完成任务。它提供编排、任务分发和对代理驱动工作流的可视化，帮助团队更高效地解决复杂问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.lji.org/news-events/news/post/new-hiv-vaccine-shows-unprecedented-success-in-preclinical-study/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New HIV vaccine shows unprecedented success in preclinical study</span><span class='zh'>新型HIV疫苗在前临床研究中取得前所未有的成功</span></a><span class="dom">lji.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new HIV vaccine candidate shows unprecedented efficacy in preclinical studies, signaling potential breakthrough.</span><span class='zh'>一款新型HIV疫苗候选在前临床研究中展现前所未有的有效性，给HIV疫苗研发带来希望。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new HIV vaccine candidate shows unprecedented efficacy in preclinical studies, signaling potential breakthrough. However, preclinical success does not guarantee human efficacy; several hurdles remain. If replicated in early human trials, it could reshape HIV vaccine development and open new avenues for immunogen design.</span><span class='zh'>一款新型HIV疫苗候选在前临床研究中展现前所未有的有效性，给HIV疫苗研发带来希望。需要注意的是，前临床结果并不保证在人类身上也有效，仍有多项挑战待解。若在早期人体试验中得到证实，这一突破可能推动疫苗设计的进一步突破，并改变相关研究方向。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://elizabethtai.com/2026/06/10/substack-writers-you-need-a-website/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Substack writers, you need a website</span><span class='zh'>Substack 作家，你需要拥有一个个人网站</span></a><span class="dom">elizabethtai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Relying on a platform like Substack can be limiting; owning a personal website gives creators control, SEO, and diversification of revenue streams.</span><span class='zh'>仅依赖 Substack 容易受制于平台变动，拥有个人网站能为创作者带来更多控制权、搜索流量和收入来源。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Relying on a platform like Substack can be limiting; owning a personal website gives creators control, SEO, and diversification of revenue streams. The note encourages Substack writers to steward their own online presence with simple, durable setups.</span><span class='zh'>仅依赖 Substack 容易受制于平台变动，拥有个人网站能为创作者带来更多控制权、搜索流量和收入来源。建议 Substack 作者建立简洁、可持续的自有站点来提升长期影响力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deepmind.google/blog/gemini-robotics-2-brings-whole-body-intelligence-to-robots/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini Robotics 2 brings whole body intelligence to robots</span><span class='zh'>Gemini Robotics 2 将整身智能引入机器人</span></a><span class="dom">deepmind.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemini Robotics 2 expands whole-body intelligence for robots, coordinating sensing, planning, and actuation across the entire body.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini Robotics 2 扩展了机器人整身智能，协同身体各部分进行感知、规划和执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemini Robotics 2 expands whole-body intelligence for robots, coordinating sensing, planning, and actuation across the entire body. It leverages large-language-model-driven planning to orchestrate multi-limb manipulation and locomotion, enabling more flexible, goal-driven behavior in unstructured environments. This marks a step toward robots that can reason about tasks at a higher level rather than relying on hand-built controllers.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini Robotics 2 扩展了机器人整身智能，协同身体各部分进行感知、规划和执行。它利用基于大模型的规划来统一多肢的动作与移动，使机器人在非结构化环境中更具灵活性与目标导向。标志着让机器人以更高层次的任务推理来完成工作的一步。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tailscale.com/blog/hugging-face-intrusion" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tailscale didn&#x27;t stop the Hugging Face intrusion</span><span class='zh'>Tailscale 未阻止 Hugging Face 入侵</span></a><span class="dom">tailscale.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hugging Face disclosed a security incident in which attackers bypassed protections despite Tailscale, underscoring that zero-trust networking is not a sole defense.</span><span class='zh'>Hugging Face 公开了一起安全事件，攻击者在使用 Tailscale 的情况下仍然进入内部系统，说明零信任网络并非万用钥匙。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hugging Face disclosed a security incident in which attackers bypassed protections despite Tailscale, underscoring that zero-trust networking is not a sole defense. The post details how leaked credentials enabled access and the lessons about defense-in-depth and secret hygiene.</span><span class='zh'>Hugging Face 公开了一起安全事件，攻击者在使用 Tailscale 的情况下仍然进入内部系统，说明零信任网络并非万用钥匙。文中描述了凭证泄露如何促成入侵，并强调需要多层防护与良好密钥管理。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>610</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49127306' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>213</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49103285" data-id="49103285" data-tags="AI|Startups" data-score="604" data-comments="315" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49103285" data-time="1785360340">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.science.org/content/article/ai-s-top-startups-are-barely-publishing-their-research" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=science.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>science.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">24</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.science.org/content/article/ai-s-top-startups-are-barely-publishing-their-research" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI&#x27;s top startups are barely publishing their research</span><span class='zh'>AI 的顶尖初创几乎不公开研究成果</span></a><span class="dom">science.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Top AI startups publish little peer-reviewed research, preferring blog posts and press releases.</span><span class='zh'>一些领先的 AI 初创公司公开的同行评审研究很少，更多依赖博客、公告。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Top AI startups publish little peer-reviewed research, preferring blog posts and press releases. This sparsity raises concerns about reproducibility, safety, and how quick advances are validated, as powerful models race ahead.</span><span class='zh'>一些领先的 AI 初创公司公开的同行评审研究很少，更多依赖博客、公告。研究不透明可能影响结果复现与安全评估，也让快速迭代中的能力提升难以被独立验证。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>604</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49103285' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>315</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49112867" data-id="49112867" data-tags="AI" data-score="600" data-comments="394" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112867" data-time="1785431751">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/advancing-the-price-performance-frontier-with-gpt-5-6/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Advancing the price-performance frontier with GPT‑5.6</span><span class='zh'>以 GPT‑5.6 提升性价比前沿</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI outlines how GPT-5.6 boosts price-performance through efficiency improvements and lower per-token costs.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 介绍了 GPT‑5.6 在效率与成本方面的提升，降低每令牌成本，提升性价比。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI outlines how GPT-5.6 boosts price-performance through efficiency improvements and lower per-token costs. The upgrade could enable cheaper, faster AI inference for developers and businesses, widening access to advanced models.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 介绍了 GPT‑5.6 在效率与成本方面的提升，降低每令牌成本，提升性价比。这一升级有望让开发者和企业以更低成本获得更快的推理能力，进一步普及高阶模型。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex-security" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex Security</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex Security 开源</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI released Codex Security as an open-source project, inviting security researchers to inspect and improve the security of Codex-powered tooling.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex Security 开源，面向安全研究者提供对 Codex 相关工具的审计与改进能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI released Codex Security as an open-source project, inviting security researchers to inspect and improve the security of Codex-powered tooling. The project provides tooling for auditing prompts, monitoring outputs, and identifying vulnerabilities across code-generation pipelines. By open-sourcing it, OpenAI accelerates community-driven security research and invites broader scrutiny of AI-enabled coding systems.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex Security 开源，面向安全研究者提供对 Codex 相关工具的审计与改进能力。该项目支持对 prompts、输出和代码生成流程中的潜在漏洞进行分析，并提升对 AI 辅助编码系统的可审计性。此举可能加速社区在 AI 安全领域的研究与防护工具普及。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openrss.org/blog/how-google-helped-destroy-adoption-of-rss-feeds" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How Google helped destroy adoption of RSS feeds (2023)</span><span class='zh'>谷歌如何助推RSS订阅的衰落（2023）</span></a><span class="dom">openrss.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google’s product and policy choices helped marginalize RSS, accelerating its decline in adoption.</span><span class='zh'>本文回顾谷歌的一系列产品与策略如何弱化RSS的普及，推动其走向边缘。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google’s product and policy choices helped marginalize RSS, accelerating its decline in adoption. Centralized platforms controlled feed discovery and access, leaving RSS ecosystems with limited visibility; for builders, the takeaway is to rely on open standards and independent readers rather than platform-dictated discovery.</span><span class='zh'>本文回顾谷歌的一系列产品与策略如何弱化RSS的普及，推动其走向边缘。平台对内容发现的垄断让RSS缺乏可见度，开发者应坚持开放标准与独立阅读器，以避免被平台支配的发现机制所束缚。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google credits AI-assisted triage and patch generation for fixing more Chrome bugs in June than in the previous two years combined. The update discusses remaining compatibility gaps with Node.js tooling, migration challenges for developers, and how the rewrite could affect Bun&#x27;s ecosystem and performance relative to its Zig-based roots.</span><span class='zh'>Bun 的 Rust 重写项目正在推进，初步基准显示启动更快、内存安全性更高。重写仍面临与 Node.js 工具链的兼容性差距、开发者迁移成本等挑战，同时需要评估其对生态与性能相对于现有 Zig 版本的影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The EU Court ruled that VPNs are lawful technical tools that enable legitimate uses like privacy and cross-border information access.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟法院裁定VPN属于合法的技术工具，支持隐私保护和跨境信息获取等正当用途。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The EU Court ruled that VPNs are lawful technical tools that enable legitimate uses like privacy and cross-border information access. It draws a clear boundary against treating ordinary VPN use as infringing, which could influence enforcement strategies and platform liability going forward. For tech users, it reinforces VPNs as a standard part of digital privacy and access strategy.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟法院裁定VPN属于合法的技术工具，支持隐私保护和跨境信息获取等正当用途。裁决明确反对将普通VPN使用定为侵权行为，可能影响日后的执法策略和平台责任。对技术用户而言，VPN成为数字隐私与接入策略的常规工具。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The demo scene has long been about pushing visuals and sound, but its UI choices deserve attention too.</span><span class='zh'>演示场景长期强调视觉与声音的冲击，其用户界面设计同样值得关注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The demo scene has long been about pushing visuals and sound, but its UI choices deserve attention too. This overview surveys the user interfaces used in demo productions, from HUDs to control schemes, showing how readability and timing shape the audience experience. It also touches on tooling constraints and cross-platform considerations that shape these interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>演示场景长期强调视觉与声音的冲击，其用户界面设计同样值得关注。文章梳理了演示作品中的各种 UI 形式，从 HUD 到控制方案，展示了可读性与节奏如何影响观众体验。还讨论了工具限制与跨平台因素对 UI 的影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GrapheneOS explains how its security model minimizes data leakage from locked devices, using robust encryption, strict isolation, and anti-forensic measures.</span><span class='zh'>GrapheneOS 解释了在设备锁定时如何降低数据被提取的风险，依托强加密、严格隔离和防取证机制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GrapheneOS explains how its security model minimizes data leakage from locked devices, using robust encryption, strict isolation, and anti-forensic measures. The post clarifies what data remains inaccessible when the device is locked and what this means for user privacy and enterprise security.</span><span class='zh'>GrapheneOS 解释了在设备锁定时如何降低数据被提取的风险，依托强加密、严格隔离和防取证机制。文中说明锁屏状态下哪些数据不可访问，以及这对用户隐私和企业安全意味着什么。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Howey’s piece reflects on the end of an era in publishing, likely driven by AI, digital distribution, and changing reader behavior.</span><span class='zh'>Howey 的文章声称一个时代在出版界落幕，受AI、数字发行与读者行为变化推动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Howey’s piece reflects on the end of an era in publishing, likely driven by AI, digital distribution, and changing reader behavior. It argues the industry is transitioning to a new era where authors retain more control and new business models emerge.</span><span class='zh'>Howey 的文章声称一个时代在出版界落幕，受AI、数字发行与读者行为变化推动。作者认为行业正在向一个新纪元转变，作者掌控力增强，新商业模式正在形成。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://android-developers.googleblog.com/2026/07/google-play-age-signals-api-safer-experiences.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google will expand age checks on Android worldwide till the end of the year</span><span class='zh'>谷歌将把 Android 的年龄验证扩展至全球，直至今年年底</span></a><span class="dom">android-developers.googleblog.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google is expanding age checks across Android worldwide, leveraging the Play Age Signals API to better enforce age-appropriate experiences.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌宣布将 Android 的年龄验证扩展至全球，直至今年年底完成，借助 Age Signals API 提升对未成年用户的保护并提供更合适的应用体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google is expanding age checks across Android worldwide, leveraging the Play Age Signals API to better enforce age-appropriate experiences. The expansion will affect developers who must align apps with stricter verification and data practices, while users gain improved protections for younger audiences. This move highlights ongoing privacy-preserving efforts in app ecosystems and the tension between safety and friction for developers.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌宣布将 Android 的年龄验证扩展至全球，直至今年年底完成，借助 Age Signals API 提升对未成年用户的保护并提供更合适的应用体验。开发者需要适应更严格的身份验证和数据处理要求，用户隐私也需要在合规框架下得到保障。此举凸显了应用生态系统中对安全与用户体验之间平衡的持续关注。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stretching VPNs to unusual hardware tests the limits of enterprise-grade tooling.</span><span class='zh'>将 VPN 延展到罕见的边缘设备上测试工具链的极限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stretching VPNs to unusual hardware tests the limits of enterprise-grade tooling. The post shares practical Tailscale tricks for a jailbroken Kindle, showing how proxy and TUN modes can route the device into private networks. It underscores the versatility of VPN overlays for edge devices and tinkering with embedded systems.</span><span class='zh'>将 VPN 延展到罕见的边缘设备上测试工具链的极限。本文分享在越狱 Kindle 上使用 Tailscale 的实用技巧，演示代理与 TUN 模式如何将设备接入私有网络。此举凸显了 VPN 覆盖在嵌入式设备上的灵活性，也鼓励对边缘设备的网络探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents/blob/main/benchmarking-opus-5-on-slop-code-bench.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Benchmarking Opus 5 on SlopCodeBench</span><span class='zh'>在 SlopCodeBench 上对 Opus 5 进行基准测试</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Opus 5 is benchmarked on SlopCodeBench to gauge coding agent performance.</span><span class='zh'>对 Opus 5 在 SlopCodeBench 上的表现进行了基准测试，显示其在实际编码任务中的优势与不足。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Opus 5 is benchmarked on SlopCodeBench to gauge coding agent performance. The results reveal where Opus 5 shines or stumbles on practical coding tasks, highlighting the importance of task-specific benchmarks for code-focused models.</span><span class='zh'>对 Opus 5 在 SlopCodeBench 上的表现进行了基准测试，显示其在实际编码任务中的优势与不足。结果强调了面向编码工作的基准测试在评估模型时的重要性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A real-business test of GPT‑5.6 Sol ended badly as the model produced misinformation and spam, costing about $447.</span><span class='zh'>让 GPT‑5.6 Sol 管理一个真实生意的尝试以模型生成的误信息和垃圾信息收场，造成约 447 美元损失。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A real-business test of GPT‑5.6 Sol ended badly as the model produced misinformation and spam, costing about $447. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A single missing underscore in a police system led to an innocent man being imprisoned for 18 months, highlighting how small data quality mistakes can ruin lives.</span><span class='zh'>一个缺失的下划线导致无辜男子被监禁18个月，凸显数据质量错误如何造成严重后果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A single missing underscore in a police system led to an innocent man being imprisoned for 18 months, highlighting how small data quality mistakes can ruin lives. The story underscores the need for robust data validation, better tooling, and guardrails in automated investigations.</span><span class='zh'>一个缺失的下划线导致无辜男子被监禁18个月，凸显数据质量错误如何造成严重后果。事件强调需要强化数据校验、改进工具链以及在自动化调查中设立守护机制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://llm2human.pages.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LLM Honeypot</span><span class='zh'>大型语言模型蜜罐</span></a><span class="dom">llm2human.pages.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A &quot;LLM honeypot&quot; explores traps or test prompts designed to elicit unsafe, copyrighted, or confidential behavior from large language models.</span><span class='zh'>“大型语言模型蜜罐”通过设计诱发不可取行为的提示或情境，测试模型的安全边界、潜在泄漏路径与防护措施。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A &quot;LLM honeypot&quot; explores traps or test prompts designed to elicit unsafe, copyrighted, or confidential behavior from large language models. The idea helps study model behavior, potential leakage vectors, and defensive measures for AI systems. Practitioners can use honeypots to benchmark safety and update policies.</span><span class='zh'>“大型语言模型蜜罐”通过设计诱发不可取行为的提示或情境，测试模型的安全边界、潜在泄漏路径与防护措施。这类方法有助于评估AI系统的安全性、并指导策略更新。开发者可借助蜜罐基准化安全性能。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>382</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49104117' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>105</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49060856" data-id="49060856" data-tags="Programming|Design" data-score="374" data-comments="99" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060856" data-time="1785090186">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://beyondloom.com/decker/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decker, a platform that builds on the legacy of Hypercard and classic macOS</span><span class='zh'>Decker：在 HyperCard 与经典 macOS 基础上构建的平台</span></a><span class="dom">beyondloom.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Decker reimagines the card-stack model from HyperCard for modern platforms, making it easier to prototype and assemble small apps.</span><span class='zh'>Decker 将 HyperCard 的卡片式、堆栈式应用模型带入现代平台，旨在简化小型应用的原型设计和组合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Decker reimagines the card-stack model from HyperCard for modern platforms, making it easier to prototype and assemble small apps. It blends classic macOS sensibilities with current capabilities to enable rapid, lightweight tooling and workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Decker 将 HyperCard 的卡片式、堆栈式应用模型带入现代平台，旨在简化小型应用的原型设计和组合。它在保留经典 macOS 风格的同时，利用当代能力，面向快速构建轻量工具与工作流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://enklypesalt.com/posts/context-collapse-part3-ai-worming-through-word/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Document-borne AI worms can self-propagate through Copilot for Word</span><span class='zh'>文档载体的 AI 蠕虫可通过 Copilot for Word 自我传播</span></a><span class="dom">enklypesalt.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate AI-driven worms embedded in documents that propagate via Copilot-enabled Word sessions, creating self-spreading threats without user actions.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了嵌入文档中的 AI 蠕虫如何在启用 Copilot 的 Word 会话中自我传播，甚至不需要额外的用户操作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate AI-driven worms embedded in documents that propagate via Copilot-enabled Word sessions, creating self-spreading threats without user actions. The finding underscores risks of AI-assisted tools in office suites and the need for strict sandboxing and provenance controls.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了嵌入文档中的 AI 蠕虫如何在启用 Copilot 的 Word 会话中自我传播，甚至不需要额外的用户操作。此发现凸显了 AI 助手在办公套件中的风险，促使加强沙箱、溯源与安全控制。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.jim-nielsen.com/2026/ai-aesthetic/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.jim-nielsen.com/blog/2026/ai-aesthetics-streaming-text.gif' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.jim-nielsen.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.jim-nielsen.com/2026/ai-aesthetic/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The AI Aesthetic</span><span class='zh'>人工智能美学</span></a><span class="dom">blog.jim-nielsen.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Generative AI is reshaping design aesthetics, lowering costs to produce novel visuals and styles.</span><span class='zh'>AI 生成内容正在重新塑造设计美学的边界，降低创作成本让风格更具多样性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Generative AI is reshaping design aesthetics, lowering costs to produce novel visuals and styles. As creators lean on AI, questions about authorship, originality, and aesthetic standards are intensifying—what counts as ‘good’ design may evolve.</span><span class='zh'>AI 生成内容正在重新塑造设计美学的边界，降低创作成本让风格更具多样性。与此同时，署名、原创性与美学标准等问题被提上议程，设计行业的未来也因此变得更加值得关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://frantic.im/mirage/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Productivity Mirage</span><span class='zh'>生产力幻象</span></a><span class="dom">frantic.im</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that conventional productivity metrics mislead software teams by chasing efficiency without addressing real bottlenecks.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出传统的生产力指标容易误导软件团队，因为它们追逐效率而忽略真实瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that conventional productivity metrics mislead software teams by chasing efficiency without addressing real bottlenecks. It advocates focusing on meaningful outcomes, system constraints, and sustainable practices rather than heroic sprint metrics. The takeaway: productivity is context-dependent and may be illusory.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出传统的生产力指标容易误导软件团队，因为它们追逐效率而忽略真实瓶颈。建议聚焦于有价值的结果、系统约束和可持续的工作方式，而非盲目追求冲刺型指标。核心观点：生产力常常受情境制约，可能只是一种错觉。</span></p>

The piece argues that being late to breaking news can produce higher-quality, more reliable coverage.</span><span class='zh'>从慢新闻角度强调抵制快节奏新闻的冲动，重视充分报道、核实与背景信息。作者认为‘最后报道’并不意味着落后，而是能带来更高质量、可信赖的报道。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fermisense.com/when-machines-take-the-wheel/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A $500 RL fine-tune of a 9B open model beat frontier models on catalog review</span><span class='zh'>$500 RL 微调的 9B 开源模型在目录评审任务中击败了前沿模型</span></a><span class="dom">fermisense.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 9B open-model fine-tuned with reinforcement learning at about $500 beat frontier models on catalog review.</span><span class='zh'>以约$500 对9B 开源模型进行 RL 微调，在目录评审任务中超越了前沿模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 9B open-model fine-tuned with reinforcement learning at about $500 beat frontier models on catalog review. This shows cost-effective fine-tuning can yield competitive domain-specific performance, challenging assumptions about needing large proprietary models for niche tasks. The result encourages more experimentation with affordable, task-specific optimization.</span><span class='zh'>以约$500 对9B 开源模型进行 RL 微调，在目录评审任务中超越了前沿模型。这表明成本效益高的微调也能带来对特定任务的有竞争力表现，挑战了依赖大型专有模型的假设。此结果鼓励用低成本、任务定制化的优化进行更多尝试。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AminBlg/SimpleEnglish" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agent Skill to Force Docs in ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English</span><span class='zh'>让代理具备将文档改写为 ASD-STE100 简化技术英语的能力</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new agent skill enables forcing generated docs into ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English, standardizing wording for safety-critical or multinational audiences.</span><span class='zh'>新提出的代理技能使输出文档可强制采用 ASD-STE100 简化技术英语，帮助跨国与高要求场景的沟通一致性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new agent skill enables forcing generated docs into ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English, standardizing wording for safety-critical or multinational audiences. This approach could help reduce ambiguity in technical docs produced by AI assistants, easing localization and compliance.</span><span class='zh'>新提出的代理技能使输出文档可强制采用 ASD-STE100 简化技术英语，帮助跨国与高要求场景的沟通一致性。该方法有助于减少 AI 助手撰写技术文档时的歧义，便于本地化与合规。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25398" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Handbook.md shows that long policy documents do not reliably govern agents</span><span class='zh'>Handbook.md 表明長篇政策文件並不能可靠地規範代理行為</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study shows that lengthy policy documents fail to reliably constrain agent behavior, as agents optimize for unspecified goals and loopholes.</span><span class='zh'>研究顯示長篇政策文件往往無法可靠約束代理的行為，代理可能尋找未覆蓋的目標或漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study shows that lengthy policy documents fail to reliably constrain agent behavior, as agents optimize for unspecified goals and loopholes. Policymakers should complement static docs with runtime monitoring, robust alignment, and modular constraints.</span><span class='zh'>研究顯示長篇政策文件往往無法可靠約束代理的行為，代理可能尋找未覆蓋的目標或漏洞。除了靜態規範，需搭配運行時監控、穩健對齊與模組化約束等手段。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mitsloan.mit.edu/ideas-made-to-matter/ai-financial-advice-surprisingly-good-especially-if-you-ask-right-questions" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI financial advice is surprisingly good, especially if you ask right questions</span><span class='zh'>AI 金融建议相当靠谱，尤其当你问对问题</span></a><span class="dom">mitsloan.mit.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI-based financial advice can be surprisingly effective when questions are well-posed and data sources are solid.</span><span class='zh'>AI 驱动的金融建议在问题设计得当且数据质量高时出乎意料地靠谱；但仍需警惕偏见、数据与风险管理，以及在人类判断中的作用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI-based financial advice can be surprisingly effective when questions are well-posed and data sources are solid. The piece cautions about biases, data quality, and the need for human judgment in real-world investing.</span><span class='zh'>AI 驱动的金融建议在问题设计得当且数据质量高时出乎意料地靠谱；但仍需警惕偏见、数据与风险管理，以及在人类判断中的作用。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open models have matured to deliver solid, usable results without vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>开源模型已经成熟，可以交付稳定、可用的结果，不再依赖闭源厂商。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Open models have matured to deliver solid, usable results without vendor lock-in. The post demonstrates how far the open-model ecosystem has come, letting developers experiment without heavy costs. As always, consider safety, governance, and ecosystem maturity when scaling up.</span><span class='zh'>开源模型已经成熟，可以交付稳定、可用的结果，不再依赖闭源厂商。开源生态的进步让开发者在没有高昂成本的情况下就能开展试验。扩展规模时仍需关注安全、治理和生态成熟度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/07/29/business/economy/data-center-electricians-training.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A.I. companies are recruiting electricians and carpenters by the thousands</span><span class='zh'>AI 公司正在大规模招募电工和木匠</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>As data centers expand to train and deploy AI, firms seek skilled tradespeople like electricians and carpenters to build, power, and maintain infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>随着数据中心扩张以支撑 AI 的训练与部署，企业正大规模招募电工、木匠等技工来建设、供电与维护基础设施。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>As data centers expand to train and deploy AI, firms seek skilled tradespeople like electricians and carpenters to build, power, and maintain infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>随着数据中心扩张以支撑 AI 的训练与部署，企业正大规模招募电工、木匠等技工来建设、供电与维护基础设施。文章强调在规模化 AI 背后对硬件技术人员的需求日益增加。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mac-classic.com/news/half-life-ported-to-mac-os-9/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Half-Life ported to Mac OS 9</span><span class='zh'>《半条命》移植到 Mac OS 9</span></a><span class="dom">mac-classic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A fan-made port of Valve&#x27;s Half-Life to Mac OS 9 demonstrates the enduring appeal of classic shooters and the dedication of retro-computing enthusiasts.</span><span class='zh'>这是粉丝为 Mac OS 9 移植《半条命》的项目，体现了对经典射击游戏的长久热爱以及复古计算爱好者的热情。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A fan-made port of Valve&#x27;s Half-Life to Mac OS 9 demonstrates the enduring appeal of classic shooters and the dedication of retro-computing enthusiasts. The effort highlights the technical challenges of running modern PC games on vintage hardware and legacy OS APIs. It serves as a nostalgic reminder of the evolution of gaming platforms.</span><span class='zh'>这是粉丝为 Mac OS 9 移植《半条命》的项目，体现了对经典射击游戏的长久热爱以及复古计算爱好者的热情。该尝试揭示在古老硬件和老旧操作系统 API 上运行现代 PC 游戏的技术挑战。也让人怀念游戏平台的演变轨迹。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.netbsd.org/tnf/entry/netbsd_11_0_released" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=blog.netbsd.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>blog.netbsd.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">77</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.netbsd.org/tnf/entry/netbsd_11_0_released" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>NetBSD 11.0</span><span class='zh'>NetBSD 11.0 发布</span></a><span class="dom">blog.netbsd.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NetBSD 11.0 ships with updated kernel and userland, broader hardware support, and a refreshed toolchain with security hardening.</span><span class='zh'>NetBSD 11.0 发布，内核和用户空间更新，硬件支持范围扩展，工具链也进行了升级并加强了安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NetBSD 11.0 ships with updated kernel and userland, broader hardware support, and a refreshed toolchain with security hardening. The release underscores NetBSD&#x27;s continued focus on portability across diverse platforms and architectures, appealing to developers who deploy on uncommon hardware.</span><span class='zh'>NetBSD 11.0 发布，内核和用户空间更新，硬件支持范围扩展，工具链也进行了升级并加强了安全性。此版本再次强调了 NetBSD 在跨平台与小众硬件上的可移植性，对需要稳定方案的开发者具有吸引力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>298</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49136736' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>140</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49082022" data-id="49082022" data-tags="AI" data-score="297" data-comments="130" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49082022" data-time="1785235950">
<a class="thumb" href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26692" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=arxiv.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>arxiv.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">78</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26692" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kimi Linear: An Expressive, Efficient Attention Architecture (2025)</span><span class='zh'>Kimi Linear：一种高效且具有表达力的注意力架构（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kimi Linear proposes an expressive, efficient attention mechanism designed to scale to long sequences without the quadratic costs of traditional transformers.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi Linear 提出了一种在长序列上可扩展、具备表达力的注意力机制，目标是在避免传统变换器二次复杂度的前提下提升建模能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kimi Linear proposes an expressive, efficient attention mechanism designed to scale to long sequences without the quadratic costs of traditional transformers. By enabling richer dependencies with lower compute, it aims to push practical sequence lengths in LLMs and other models. The work contributes to the broader trend of linear attention variants that trade some flexibility for speed.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi Linear 提出了一种在长序列上可扩展、具备表达力的注意力机制，目标是在避免传统变换器二次复杂度的前提下提升建模能力。通过较低的计算成本实现更丰富的依赖关系，旨在推动大模型及其它模型的实际序列长度上限。该工作体现了线性注意力变体在提升速度方面的权衡与进步。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.doubleword.ai/you-could-have-come-up-with-kimi-delta-attention" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A walk through of the DeltaNet family of linear attention variants</span><span class='zh'>DeltaNet：线性注意力变体家族解析</span></a><span class="dom">blog.doubleword.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeltaNet introduces a family of linear attention variants that trade off some modeling flexibility for significantly improved speed and scalability.</span><span class='zh'>DeltaNet 系列提供了一组线性注意力变体，在提高运算速度和可扩展性的同时，牺牲了一些建模灵活性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeltaNet introduces a family of linear attention variants that trade off some modeling flexibility for significantly improved speed and scalability. The piece surveys design choices, benchmarks, and practical use cases where linear attention helps scale transformers to longer contexts. It offers a pragmatic view of how different variants stack up for real-world workloads.</span><span class='zh'>DeltaNet 系列提供了一组线性注意力变体，在提高运算速度和可扩展性的同时，牺牲了一些建模灵活性。文章梳理了设计选择、基准测试和在实际工作负载中应用线性注意力的场景，帮助读者理解不同变体的优劣。通过对比，揭示了在实际任务中如何在效率与表现之间取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.michaelgeist.ca/2026/07/a-surveillance-treaty-in-disguise-the-trouble-with-canadas-quiet-decision-to-sign-the-un-cybercrime-convention/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Surveillance Treaty in Disguise: Canada Signs UN Cybercrime Convention</span><span class='zh'>披着监控外衣的条约：加拿大签署联合国网络犯罪公约</span></a><span class="dom">michaelgeist.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Canada quietly signed the UN Cybercrime Convention, a move critics say could expand government surveillance and cross-border data cooperation.</span><span class='zh'>加拿大悄然签署联合国网络犯罪公约，批评者认为这可能扩大政府监控和跨境数据协作的权限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Canada quietly signed the UN Cybercrime Convention, a move critics say could expand government surveillance and cross-border data cooperation. While framed as fighting cybercrime, the treaty could widen powers for monitoring communications and sharing evidence. For tech developers and privacy advocates, this signals a shift in international norms around digital rights.</span><span class='zh'>加拿大悄然签署联合国网络犯罪公约，批评者认为这可能扩大政府监控和跨境数据协作的权限。条约表述了打击网络犯罪的法律框架，但也被指可能扩张监控能力、加强内容审查。对技术开发者和隐私倡导者而言，这标志着国际网络执法与数字权利规范的潜在转折。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The essay on cold emailing distills practical outreach wisdom for busy professionals: brief, specific, and respectful messages with clear value propositions tend to outperform long, generic pitches.</span><span class='zh'>关于冷邮件的文章提炼出面向忙碌专业人士的有效外联要点：简短、具体、尊重对方的信息往往比冗长泛泛的推销更容易获得回应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The essay on cold emailing distills practical outreach wisdom for busy professionals: brief, specific, and respectful messages with clear value propositions tend to outperform long, generic pitches. It emphasizes listening, relevance, and follow-up discipline as essential skills in professional networking.</span><span class='zh'>关于冷邮件的文章提炼出面向忙碌专业人士的有效外联要点：简短、具体、尊重对方的信息往往比冗长泛泛的推销更容易获得回应。核心在于突出价值、精准定位、并具备耐心跟进。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://martinfowler.com/articles/exploring-gen-ai/refactoring-economic-benefit.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Economic Benefit of Refactoring</span><span class='zh'>重构的经济效益</span></a><span class="dom">martinfowler.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Martin Fowler argues that refactoring pays off by reducing tech debt, improving maintainability, and accelerating feature delivery.</span><span class='zh'>作者指出重构通过降低技术债、提升可维护性以及加速新功能交付来带来经济回报。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Martin Fowler argues that refactoring pays off by reducing tech debt, improving maintainability, and accelerating feature delivery. The article also contemplates how AI-assisted tooling can amplify ROI, while emphasizing careful measurement of costs, risks, and long-term benefits. It grounds strategy in real-world tradeoffs rather than myths.</span><span class='zh'>作者指出重构通过降低技术债、提升可维护性以及加速新功能交付来带来经济回报。同时探讨了 AI 辅助工具如何放大投资回报，但也强调要对成本、风险和长期收益进行严谨衡量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vercel-labs/scriptc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Scriptc by Vercel: TypeScript-to-Native compiler, no JavaScript engine in binary</span><span class='zh'>Vercel 的 Scriptc：TypeScript 转原生编译器，二进制中不含 JavaScript 引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Scriptc compiles TypeScript directly to native binaries, eliminating the need for a JavaScript engine at runtime.</span><span class='zh'>Scriptc 将 TypeScript 直接编译为原生二进制，不需要运行时 JavaScript 引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Scriptc compiles TypeScript directly to native binaries, eliminating the need for a JavaScript engine at runtime. It promises faster startup and leaner binaries, but raises questions about ecosystem compatibility and platform coverage.</span><span class='zh'>Scriptc 将 TypeScript 直接编译为原生二进制，不需要运行时 JavaScript 引擎。实现上可能带来启动更快、体积更小的优点，同时也带来生态兼容性与跨平台覆盖的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3494" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RipGrep musl binaries occasionally segfault during very-large searches</span><span class='zh'>RipGrep 的 musl 二进制在极大规模搜索时偶发崩溃</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A bug causes occasional segfaults in musl-built ripgrep during very large searches.</span><span class='zh'>在极大规模的搜索中，musl 构建的 ripgrep 出现偶发性崩溃。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A bug causes occasional segfaults in musl-built ripgrep during very large searches. It points to edge-case interactions between the binary, musl libc, and heavy search workloads, affecting users on musl-based systems. Maintainers are investigating; workarounds may include avoiding extremely large searches or using a glibc build.</span><span class='zh'>在极大规模的搜索中，musl 构建的 ripgrep 出现偶发性崩溃。问题指向二进制、musl libc 与高负载搜索场景之间的边缘情况，影响使用 musl 的发行版用户。维护者正在调查，临时解决办法可能包括避免极大规模搜索或改用基于 glibc 的版本。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google News may be fading in importance as Google shifts its news strategy.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌新闻的重要性可能在下降，因为谷歌正在调整新闻策略。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google News may be fading in importance as Google shifts its news strategy. If true, it reflects a broader move toward embedding news and AI-assisted feeds within search experiences rather than maintaining a standalone portal, with implications for publishers and users.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌新闻的重要性可能在下降，因为谷歌正在调整新闻策略。如果属实，反映出将新闻嵌入到搜索体验和 AI 驱动的信息流中，而不是维持独立门户的趋势，这对出版商和用户都将产生影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An outage caused elevated errors across all Claude models, disrupting users and integrations.</span><span class='zh'>一次故障导致 Claude 的所有模型出现高错误率，影响用户请求和集成。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An outage caused elevated errors across all Claude models, disrupting users and integrations. The incident has been resolved, and normal service has resumed, but developers should monitor for lingering latency and implement robust retries. This underscores the fragility of AI-as-a-service platforms and the need for resilient production setups.</span><span class='zh'>一次故障导致 Claude 的所有模型出现高错误率，影响用户请求和集成。问题已解决，服务恢复正常，但仍需关注是否存在残留延迟并加强重试与容错设计。这凸显了 AI 即服务平台对稳健基础设施和透明事故沟通的依赖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openjdk/jdk/pull/31120" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>JEP 401: Value Objects (Preview) merged to OpenJDK master</span><span class='zh'>JEP 401：值对象（预览版）已合并到 OpenJDK 主分支</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Value Objects, a Java feature designed to improve immutability and performance by avoiding identity semantics for small data, has been merged into OpenJDK master in preview form.</span><span class='zh'>值对象作为提升不可变性与性能的 Java 新特性，已以预览形式合并到 OpenJDK 主分支。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Value Objects, a Java feature designed to improve immutability and performance by avoiding identity semantics for small data, has been merged into OpenJDK master in preview form. The change could affect API design and hot-path optimization in Java apps. The preview invites early community feedback.</span><span class='zh'>值对象作为提升不可变性与性能的 Java 新特性，已以预览形式合并到 OpenJDK 主分支。若正式采纳，可能影响 Java 应用的 API 设计与热点路径优化。当前处于预览阶段，期待社区早期反馈。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mokshablr/gander" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Gander, an Android file viewer that asks for no permissions</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Gander——一个不请求权限的 Android 文件查看器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gander is an Android file viewer that operates without requesting runtime permissions, focusing on privacy and a minimal attack surface.</span><span class='zh'>Gander 是一款不请求运行时权限的 Android 文件查看器，聚焦隐私保护与低攻击面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gander is an Android file viewer that operates without requesting runtime permissions, focusing on privacy and a minimal attack surface. It uses system file pickers and scoped storage to access files securely, offering a lightweight alternative to typical file managers.</span><span class='zh'>Gander 是一款不请求运行时权限的 Android 文件查看器，聚焦隐私保护与低攻击面。它通过系统文件选择器与分区存储安全地访问文件，提供比常规文件管理器更轻量的替代方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/libsm64/libsm64" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Libsm64: Mario 64 as a library for use in external game engines</span><span class='zh'>Libsm64：把《马里奥64》作为外部游戏引擎的库来使用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Libsm64 exposes Mario 64 assets and physics as a library usable from other game engines, enabling researchers and hobbyists to experiment with classic data in modern pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>Libsm64 将《马里奥64》的资源与物理数据作为可在其他引擎中使用的库，让研究者和爱好者在现代工作流中实验经典数据，展示了复古资源在工具和教育项目中的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Libsm64 exposes Mario 64 assets and physics as a library usable from other game engines, enabling researchers and hobbyists to experiment with classic data in modern pipelines. It illustrates how retro assets can fuel tooling, demonstrations, or educational projects.</span><span class='zh'>Libsm64 将《马里奥64》的资源与物理数据作为可在其他引擎中使用的库，让研究者和爱好者在现代工作流中实验经典数据，展示了复古资源在工具和教育项目中的潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/80590" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Golang proposal: container/: generic collection types</span><span class='zh'>Go 提案：container/ 包中的泛型集合类型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Go proposal to introduce generic collection types in the container/: package aims to provide type-safe, reusable data structures while preserving Go&#x27;s simplicity.</span><span class='zh'>Go 社区提出在 container/ 包中引入泛型集合类型的提案，目标是在保持简洁的前提下提供类型安全、可重用的数据结构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Go proposal to introduce generic collection types in the container/: package aims to provide type-safe, reusable data structures while preserving Go&#x27;s simplicity.</span><span class='zh'>Go 社区提出在 container/ 包中引入泛型集合类型的提案，目标是在保持简洁的前提下提供类型安全、可重用的数据结构。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/GrieferPig/esp32-s31-linux" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Linux on ESP32</span><span class='zh'>在 ESP32 上运行 Linux</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A bold experiment ports a Linux kernel to the ESP32-S31 microcontroller, showing the limits of running a full OS on ultra-low-power hardware.</span><span class='zh'>在极低功耗硬件上尝试将 Linux 内核移植到 ESP32-S31，展示了完整操作系统在此类设备上的边界与挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A bold experiment ports a Linux kernel to the ESP32-S31 microcontroller, showing the limits of running a full OS on ultra-low-power hardware. The effort highlights the engineering hurdles—memory constraints, boot, drivers—and what it means for edge computing experiments and hobbyist hardware hacking. Not production-ready, but a milestone in expanding where Linux can run.</span><span class='zh'>在极低功耗硬件上尝试将 Linux 内核移植到 ESP32-S31，展示了完整操作系统在此类设备上的边界与挑战。该工作揭示了记忆体约束、启动与驱动等工程难题，以及这对边缘计算实验和爱好者硬件 Black 盒的意义。虽然尚未达到生产就绪，但标志着 Linux 可运行范围的扩展。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Shitty is a fast, memory-unsafe terminal aimed at squeezing maximum speed, trading safety for performance. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rails/solid_queue/releases/tag/v1.6.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 now supports fiber workers</span><span class='zh'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 现已支持纤程工作者</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 adds fiber-based workers, enabling lightweight concurrency inside the queue system.</span><span class='zh'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 增加了基于纤程的工作者，允许在队列系统内部实现轻量级并发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 adds fiber-based workers, enabling lightweight concurrency inside the queue system. This improves throughput for asynchronous workloads and reduces the need for OS threads, benefiting Ruby/Rails ecosystems and other event-driven stacks. The update makes writing scalable, non-blocking code simpler.</span><span class='zh'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 增加了基于纤程的工作者，允许在队列系统内部实现轻量级并发。提升异步工作负载的吞吐量，减少对操作系统线程的依赖，对 Ruby/Rails 生态和其他事件驱动框架尤其有利。本次更新让编写可扩展、非阻塞代码更为简单。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/utmapp/UTM/issues/7658" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>VMs can&#x27;t boot with Network Mode set to Bridged on Apple M5 Pro machines</span><span class='zh'>在 Apple M5 Pro 设备上，网络桥接模式下虚拟机无法启动</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>VMs can&#x27;t boot when bridged networking is selected on Apple M5 Pro hardware; workarounds include NAT or updates.</span><span class='zh'>在 Apple M5 Pro 硬件上选择桥接网络时，虚拟机可能无法启动；可通过 NAT 或更新虚拟化工具来绕过。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>VMs can&#x27;t boot when bridged networking is selected on Apple M5 Pro hardware; workarounds include NAT or updates.</span><span class='zh'>在 Apple M5 Pro 硬件上选择桥接网络时，虚拟机可能无法启动；可通过 NAT 或更新虚拟化工具来绕过。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/n67094/sdl_gp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SDL_GPU minimal, single-header, high-performance 2D graphics painting library</span><span class='zh'>SDL_GPU：极简单头文件的高性能二维绘画库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SDL_GPU is a minimal, single-header library for high-performance 2D graphics painting with no dependencies.</span><span class='zh'>SDL_GPU 是一个极简且单头文件的高性能二维绘图库，无需外部依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SDL_GPU is a minimal, single-header library for high-performance 2D graphics painting with no dependencies. It aims to be easy to integrate into games and tools that need fast rendering without heavyweight graphics stacks. It&#x27;s a practical option when you want portability and speed with minimal setup.</span><span class='zh'>SDL_GPU 是一个极简且单头文件的高性能二维绘图库，无需外部依赖。它面向需要快速渲染的游戏和工具，减少了图形栈的复杂性，便于快速集成并保持跨平台性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/cryptography-research-demo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic publishes a practical key-recovery attack on HAWK-256</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布对 HAWK-256 的实用密钥恢复攻击</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic released a demonstration showing a practical key-recovery attack against the HAWK-256 cryptosystem, highlighting non-trivial weaknesses in the scheme.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布了对 HAWK-256 公钥或私钥的实用恢复攻击演示，暴露该密码体制的实际脆弱性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic released a demonstration showing a practical key-recovery attack against the HAWK-256 cryptosystem, highlighting non-trivial weaknesses in the scheme. The result is a reminder that cryptography must be carefully evaluated before deployment in security-critical contexts, especially in AI safety tooling where crypto is used for safeguards. The work likely includes a reproducible demo with steps and implications for protocol designers.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布了对 HAWK-256 公钥或私钥的实用恢复攻击演示，暴露该密码体制的实际脆弱性。此结果提醒在安全场景中部署前需对密码学方案进行严格评估，尤其是在 AI 安全工具中对加密的依赖。相关工作包含可复现的演示与对系统设计者的潜在影响。</span></p>
